### getAwaitingJobsWithInfo()
#### Timings
  - invocations: 197
  - total duration: PT11.210229655S
  - avg duration: PT0.056904719S
  - min duration: PT0.006213793S
  - max duration: PT0.206986804S

#### Queries
##### Query 1
 -  Query identifier: `select ns.*, j.jobAsJson from(     select j.id, j.awaitingOn AS awaitedJobId, p.state AS awaitedJobState         FROM jobrunr_jobs j INNER JOIN jobrunr_jobs p ON p.id = j.awaitingOn         WHERE j.state = 'AWAITING' AND j.rateLimiter IS NULL AND (j.awaitingOnState = p.state OR p.state  IN ('SUCCEEDED','FAILED','DELETED','NOT_APPLICABLE'))  ORDER BY j.rateLimiter ASC, j.priority ASC, j.updatedAt ASC LIMIT :limit) ns, jobrunr_jobs j WHERE j.id = ns.id`
 - Query with values: `select ns.*, j.jobAsJson from(     select j.id, j.awaitingOn AS awaitedJobId, p.state AS awaitedJobState         FROM jobrunr_jobs j INNER JOIN jobrunr_jobs p ON p.id = j.awaitingOn         WHERE j.state = 'AWAITING' AND j.rateLimiter IS NULL AND (j.awaitingOnState = p.state OR p.state  IN ('SUCCEEDED','FAILED','DELETED','NOT_APPLICABLE'))  ORDER BY j.rateLimiter ASC, j.priority ASC, j.updatedAt ASC LIMIT 1024) ns, jobrunr_jobs j WHERE j.id = ns.id`
   - timeToFirstResultSet query part timings: invocations: 197 / total duration: PT7.261015051S / avg duration: PT0.036857944S / min duration: PT0.00080349S / max duration: PT0.169910068S

### getBatchJobsWithChildStats()
#### Timings
  - invocations: 18
  - total duration: PT9.330205579S
  - avg duration: PT0.518344754S
  - min duration: PT0.14472488S
  - max duration: PT1.067313582S

#### Queries
##### Query 1
 -  Query identifier: `select bjs.*, j.jobAsJson   FROM (SELECT bj.id,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.parentJobId IS NOT NULL) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS totalChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'SUCCEEDED' OR cj.state = 'DELETED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS succeededChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'FAILED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failedChildCount,              MAX(cj.updatedAt) as lastChildUpdatedAt         FROM jobrunr_jobs cj RIGHT JOIN jobrunr_jobs bj ON bj.id = cj.parentJobId         WHERE bj.isBatch = 1 AND bj.state  IN ('PROCESSED','FAILED')        GROUP BY bj.id) bjs,      jobrunr_jobs j   WHERE j.id = bjs.id and (bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt IS NULL OR bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt >= :lastChildUpdatedAfter OR bjs.totalChildCount = bjs.succeededChildCount)`
 - Query with values: `select bjs.*, j.jobAsJson   FROM (SELECT bj.id,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.parentJobId IS NOT NULL) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS totalChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'SUCCEEDED' OR cj.state = 'DELETED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS succeededChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'FAILED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failedChildCount,              MAX(cj.updatedAt) as lastChildUpdatedAt         FROM jobrunr_jobs cj RIGHT JOIN jobrunr_jobs bj ON bj.id = cj.parentJobId         WHERE bj.isBatch = 1 AND bj.state  IN ('PROCESSED','FAILED')        GROUP BY bj.id) bjs,      jobrunr_jobs j   WHERE j.id = bjs.id and (bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt IS NULL OR bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt >= '2025-10-16 08:35:34.223' OR bjs.totalChildCount = bjs.succeededChildCount)`
   - timeToFirstResultSet query part timings: invocations: 18 / total duration: PT8.957681545S / avg duration: PT0.497648974S / min duration: PT0.142642154S / max duration: PT1.067124584S
   - prepareTime query part timings: invocations: 18 / total duration: PT0.000070673S / avg duration: PT0.000003926S / min duration: PT0.000001226S / max duration: PT0.000016953S
   - totalTime query part timings: invocations: 18 / total duration: PT8.976829155S / avg duration: PT0.49871273S / min duration: PT0.142665544S / max duration: PT1.067280035S
